\mathrm{Cov}\left(z_{i_{1};\alpha_{1}}^{(\ell+1)},\,z_{i_{2};\alpha_{2}}^{(\ell+1)}~|~\mathcal{F}^{(\ell)}\right)=\delta_{i_{1}i_{2}}\Sigma_{\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}}^{{}_{(\ell)}}=\delta_{i_{2}i_{2}}\left(C_{b}+\frac{C_{W}}{n_{\ell}}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{\ell}}\sigma\left(z_{j;\alpha_{1}}^{(\ell)}\right)\sigma\left(z_{j;\alpha_{2}}^{(\ell)}\right)\right)

that we introduced in (
3.5
). Our next result gives recursions with respect to
\ell
directly for the covariances
\kappa_{(j_{1}j_{2})(j_{1}^{\prime}j_{2}^{\prime})}^{{}_{(\ell)}}
and hence via (
10.9
) for the fourth cumulants of
z_{i;\alpha}^{(\ell)}
and its first derivatives as well.

Proposition 10.2
.

We have

\displaystyle\kappa_{(00)(00)}^{(\ell+1)}=\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{n_{\ell}}\left(\left\langle\sigma^{4}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}-\left\langle\sigma^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}^{2}\right)+\left(\chi_{||}^{(\ell)}\right)^{2}\kappa_{(00)(00)}^{(\ell)}+O(n^{-2}).

Further,

\displaystyle\kappa_{(10)(00)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{n_{\ell}}\left(\left\langle\sigma^{3}\sigma^{\prime}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}-\left\langle\sigma^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\sigma\sigma^{\prime}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right)

(10.11)

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{8}\left[2\kappa_{(00)(00)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}\sigma^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}+4\kappa_{(10)(00)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}\sigma^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right]+O(n^{-2}).

Moreover,

\displaystyle\kappa_{(10)(10)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{n_{\ell}}\left[\left\langle(\sigma\sigma^{\prime}d_{1}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}-\left\langle\sigma\sigma^{\prime}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{8}\left[2\kappa_{(00)(00)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})d_{1}z\right\rangle^{2}+8\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(10)(00)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\qquad\qquad+8\kappa_{(10)(10)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})\right\rangle_{(\ell)}^{2}\right]+O(n^{-2}).

Similarly,

\displaystyle\kappa_{(10)(20)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{n_{\ell}}\left[\left\langle(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}zd_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}-\left\langle\sigma\sigma^{\prime}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\sigma\sigma^{\prime}d_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{8}\left[2\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(00)(00)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})d_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+4\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(10)(00)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})d_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}+4\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(20)(00)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\left.8\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(10)(20)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})\right\rangle_{(\ell)}^{2}\right]+O(n^{-2}).

In addition,

\displaystyle\kappa_{(11)(00)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{n_{\ell}}\left[\left\langle(\sigma\sigma^{\prime}d_{1}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}-\left\langle\sigma^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime}d_{1}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{8}\left[2\kappa_{(00)(00)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}\sigma^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}(d_{1}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}+8\kappa_{(10)(00)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}\sigma^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\left.4\kappa_{(11)(00)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle\left\langle\partial^{2}\sigma^{2}\right\rangle\right]+O(n^{-2}).

Also,

\displaystyle\kappa_{(12)(00)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{n_{\ell}}\left[\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\sigma^{2}d_{1}zd_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}-\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}zd_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\sigma^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{8}\left[2\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(00)(00)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}zd_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}\sigma^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+4\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(10)(00)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}\sigma^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}+4\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(20)(00)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}\sigma^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\left.4\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(12)(00)}\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}\sigma^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right]+O(n^{-2}).

Additionally,

\displaystyle\kappa_{(11)(10)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{n_{\ell}}\left[\left\langle\sigma(\sigma^{\prime}d_{1}z)^{3}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}-\left\langle\sigma\sigma^{\prime}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime}d_{1}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{8}\left[2\kappa_{(00)(00)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}(d_{1}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+4\kappa_{(10)(00)}^{(\ell)}\left(\left\langle\partial(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}(d_{1}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}+2\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+16\kappa_{(10)(10)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}+4\kappa_{(11)(00)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\left.+8\kappa_{(11)(10)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right]+O(n^{-2}).

Also,

\displaystyle\kappa_{(12)(10)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{n_{\ell}}\left[\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{3}\sigma(d_{1}z)^{2}d_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}-\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}zd_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\sigma\sigma^{\prime}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{8}\left[2\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(00)(00)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}zd_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+4\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(10)(00)}\left\{\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}+\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}zd_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+4\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(20)(00)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}+4\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(12)(00)}\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+8\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(10)(10)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})\right\rangle_{(\ell)}+8\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(10)(20)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})\right\rangle_{(\ell)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\left.+8\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(12)(10)}\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right]+O(n^{-2}).

Further,

\displaystyle\kappa_{(11)(20)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{n_{\ell}}\left[\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\sigma(d_{1}z)^{2}d_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}-\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime}d_{1}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\sigma\sigma^{\prime}d_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{8}\left[2\kappa_{(00)(00)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}(d_{1}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})d_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+8\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(10)(00)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})d_{2}z\right\rangle+4\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(20)(00)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}(d_{1}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})\right\rangle_{(\ell)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+4\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(11)(00)}\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})d_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}+16\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(10)(20)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})\right\rangle_{(\ell)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\left.8\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(11)(20)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right]+O(n^{-2}).

Additionally,

\displaystyle\kappa_{(11)(11)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{n_{\ell}}\left[\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime}dz)^{4}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}-\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime}dz)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{8}\left[2\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(00)(00)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}(d_{1}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}^{2}+16\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(10)(00)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}(d_{1}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad+8\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(11)(00)}\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}(d_{1}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}+32\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(10)(10)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\left.+32\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(11)(10)}\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}+8\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(11)(11)}\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle^{2}\right].

Also,

\displaystyle\kappa_{(11)(22)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{n_{\ell}}\left[\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{4}(d_{1}zd_{2}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}-\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime}d_{1}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime}d_{2}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{8}\left[2\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(00)(00)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}(d_{1}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}(d_{2}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+8\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(10)(00)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}(d_{2}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}+8\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(20)(00)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}(d_{1}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+4\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(11)(00)}\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}(d_{2}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}+4\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(22)(00)}\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})(d_{1}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+32\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(10)(20)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+16\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(11)(20)}\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}+16\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(22)(10)}\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\left.+8\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(11)(22)}\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}^{2}\right]+O(n^{-2}).

Finally,

\displaystyle\kappa_{(12)(12)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{n_{\ell}}\left[\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{4}(d_{1}zd_{2}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}-\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}zd_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{8}\left[2\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(00)(00)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}zd_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}^{2}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+8\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(10)(00)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}zd_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}+8\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(20)(00)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}zd_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+8\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(12)(00)}\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}zd_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}+26\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(10)(20)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+8\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(10)(10)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}^{2}+8\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(20)(20)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+16\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(12)(10)}\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}+16\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(12)(20)}\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\left.+8\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(12)(12)}\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}^{2}\right]+O(n^{-2}).

Proof.

The derivations of the recursions in Proposition
10.2
are all similar. The idea is to apply Theorem
3.2
in the special case of
q_{*}=1
,
r=1
, a single network input
x_{\alpha}
, and directional derivatives

d_{j}=\partial_{x_{j;\alpha}},\qquad j=1,2.

To keep the notation as uniform as possible, we will continue to write

d_{0}=\text{ identity}

for the identity operator. We will have occasion to apply the result to compute expressions of the form

\mathbb{E}\left[f\left(d_{j}z_{i;\alpha}^{(\ell)},\,\,i=1,\ldots,m,\,j=0,1,2\right)\right],

(10.12)

where the function
f
depends on the pre-activations
z_{i;\alpha}^{{}_{(\ell)}},\,i=1,\ldots,m
at finitely many neurons and is a polynomial of total degree between
0
and
4
in the derivatives
d_{j}z_{i;\alpha}^{{}_{(\ell)}}
. Theorem
3.2
allows us to expand (
10.12
) into a perturbative series in powers of
1/n
with coefficients that are Gaussian integrals of the form

\left\langle\mathcal{P}f(d_{j}z_{i},i=1,\ldots,m,j=1,2)\right\rangle_{\kappa^{(\ell)}},

where
\mathcal{P}
is a differential operator consisting of finite sums of partial derivatives with respect to the variables
d_{j}z_{i}
, and
\left\langle\cdot\right\rangle_{\kappa^{(\ell)}}
denotes an expectation in which
d_{j}z_{i;\alpha}
are centered Gaussian with covariance

\displaystyle\delta_{i_{1}i_{2}}\kappa_{(j_{1}j_{2})}^{(\ell)}:=\mathrm{Cov}\left(d_{j_{1}}z_{i_{1}},d_{j_{2}}z_{i_{2}}^{(\ell)}\right):=\delta_{i_{1}i_{2}}\mathrm{Cov}\left(d_{j_{1}}z_{i_{1};\alpha}^{(\ell)},d_{j_{2}}z_{i_{2};\alpha}^{(\ell)}\right),\qquad j_{1},j_{2}=0,1,2.

Specifically, taking
g\equiv 1
in Theorem
3.2
we find that (
10.12
) equals

\displaystyle\left\langle f\right\rangle_{\kappa^{(\ell)}}+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}=1}^{m}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{1}^{\prime},j_{2}^{\prime}=0}^{2}\kappa_{(j_{1}j_{2})(j_{1}^{\prime}j_{2}^{\prime})}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial d_{j_{1}}z_{i}\partial d_{j_{2}}z_{i}}\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial d_{j_{1}^{\prime}}z_{i^{\prime}}\partial d_{j_{2};}z_{i^{\prime}}}f\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}+O(n^{-2})

Note that in the expectations inside the sum the integrals are with respect to a centered Gaussian with the infinite width covariance
K^{(\ell)}
defined in §
10.1
instead of the finite width covariance
\kappa^{(\ell)}
. The reason is that, as we’ll show in §
10.4
, we have
\kappa^{(\ell)}=K^{(\ell)}+O(n^{-1})
and since
\kappa_{(00)(00)}^{(\ell)}=O(n^{-1})
by Theorem
3.1
, the replacement of
\kappa^{(\ell)}
by
K^{(\ell)}
incurs an error of size
O(n^{-2})
, which we are neglecting anyway.

With that said, note that if
f
is a polynomial of degree
0
in the derivatives
d_{j}z_{i;\alpha}^{{}_{(\ell)}}
for
j=1,2
(i.e. doesn’t depends on them), then we find that (
10.12
) equals

\left\langle f\right\rangle_{\kappa^{(\ell)}}+\frac{1}{8}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}=1}^{m}\kappa_{(00)(00)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial z_{i}^{2}}\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial z_{i^{\prime}}^{2}}f\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}+O(n^{-2}).

(10.13)

If instead
f
is a polynomial of degree
1
in the derivatives
d_{j}z_{i;\alpha}^{{}_{(\ell)}}
then (
10.12
) equals (
10.13
) plus the expression

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}=1}^{m}

\displaystyle 2\kappa_{(10)(00)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\left\{\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial z_{i}\partial d_{1}z_{i}}\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial z_{i^{\prime}}^{2}}+\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial z_{i^{\prime}}\partial d_{1}z_{i^{\prime}}}\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial z_{i}^{2}}\right\}f\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}+\left(1\leftrightarrow 2\right),

(10.14)

where
\left(1\leftrightarrow 2\right)
means the same expression with
d_{1}
exchanged with
d_{2}
,
\kappa_{(10)(00)}^{{}_{(\ell)}}
replaced by
\kappa_{(20)(00)}^{{}_{(\ell)}}
, and so on. If
f
is a polynomial of degree
2
in the derivatives
d_{j}z_{i;\alpha}^{{}_{(\ell)}}
then we must add to (
10.13
) and (
10.14
) in addition

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}=1}^{m}

\displaystyle\left[\kappa_{(11)(00)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\left\{\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial d_{1}z_{i}^{2}}\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial z_{i^{\prime}}^{2}}+\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial d_{1}z_{i^{\prime}}^{2}}\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial z_{i}^{2}}\right\}f\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}+\left(1\leftrightarrow 2\right)\right.

(10.15)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle+4\kappa_{(10)(10)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial z_{i}\partial d_{1}z_{i}}\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial z_{i^{\prime}}\partial d_{1}z_{i^{\prime}}}f\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}+\left(1\leftrightarrow 2\right)

\displaystyle\left.+4\kappa_{(10)(20)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\left\{\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial z_{i}\partial d_{1}z_{i}}\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial z_{i^{\prime}}\partial d_{2}z_{i^{\prime}}}+\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial z_{i}\partial d_{2}z_{i}}\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial z_{i^{\prime}}\partial d_{1}z_{i^{\prime}}}\right\}f\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}\right]

Similarly, if
f
is a polynomial of degree
3
in the derivatives
d_{j}z_{i;\alpha}^{{}_{(\ell)}}
then we must add to (
10.13
)-(
10.15
) in also

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}=1}^{m}

\displaystyle\left[2\kappa_{(11)(10)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\left\{\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial d_{1}z_{i}^{2}}\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial z_{i^{\prime}}\partial d_{1}z_{i^{\prime}}}+\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial d_{1}z_{i^{\prime}}^{2}}\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial z_{i}\partial d_{1}z_{i}}\right\}f\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}+\left(1\leftrightarrow 2\right)\right.

(10.16)

\displaystyle+2\kappa_{(11)(20)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\left\{\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial d_{1}z_{i}^{2}}\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial z_{i^{\prime}}\partial d_{2}z_{i^{\prime}}}+\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial d_{1}z_{i^{\prime}}^{2}}\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial z_{i}\partial d_{2}z_{i}}\right\}f\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}+\left(1\leftrightarrow 2\right)

\displaystyle\left.+4\kappa_{(12)(10)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\left\{\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial d_{1}z_{i}\partial d_{2}z_{i}}\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial z_{i^{\prime}}\partial d_{1}z_{i^{\prime}}}+\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial d_{1}z_{i^{\prime}}\partial d_{2}z_{i^{\prime}}}\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial z_{i}\partial d_{1}z_{i}}\right\}f\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}+\left(1\leftrightarrow 2\right)\right].

Finally, if
f
is a polynomial of degree
4
in the derivatives
d_{j}z_{i;\alpha}^{{}_{(\ell)}}
then we must add to (
10.13
)-(
10.16
) also

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}=1}^{m}

\displaystyle\left[\kappa_{(11)(11)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\left\{\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial d_{1}z_{i}^{2}}\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial d_{1}z_{i^{\prime}}^{2}}\right\}f\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}+\left(1\leftrightarrow 2\right)\right.

(10.17)

\displaystyle+2\kappa_{(11)(12)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\left\{\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial d_{1}z_{i}^{2}}\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial d_{1}z_{i^{\prime}}\partial d_{2}z_{i^{\prime}}}+\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial d_{1}z_{i^{\prime}}^{2}}\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial d_{1}z_{i}\partial d_{2}z_{i}}\right\}f\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}+\left(1\leftrightarrow 2\right)

\displaystyle+\kappa_{(11)(22)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\left\{\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial d_{1}z_{i}^{2}}\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial d_{2}z_{i^{\prime}}^{2}}+\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial d_{1}z_{i^{\prime}}^{2}}\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial d_{2}z_{i}^{2}}\right\}f\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}

\displaystyle\left.+4\kappa_{(12)(12)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial d_{1}z_{i}\partial d_{2}z_{i}}\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial d_{1}z_{i^{\prime}}\partial d_{2}z_{i^{\prime}}}f\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}\right].

With these formulas in hand, the derivations of the recursions in Proposition
10.2
are straight forward and we provide the details for the case of
\kappa_{(10)(00)}^{{}_{(\ell+1)}}
, which already illustrates all the main ideas of the derivation. We have

\kappa_{(10)(00)}^{(\ell+1)}=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\Sigma_{\alpha\alpha}^{(\ell)}-\mathbb{E}\left[\Sigma_{\alpha\alpha}^{(\ell)}\right]\right)\cdot\left(\partial_{x_{1;\alpha_{1}}}\Sigma_{\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}}^{(\ell)}\bigg|_{\alpha_{1}=\alpha_{2}=\alpha}-\mathbb{E}\left[\partial_{x_{1;\alpha_{1}}}\Sigma_{\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}}^{(\ell)}\right]\bigg|_{\alpha_{1}=\alpha_{2}=\alpha}\right)\right].

Recall that

\Sigma_{\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}}^{(\ell)}=\frac{C_{W}}{n_{\ell}}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{\ell}}\sigma\left(z_{j;\alpha_{1}}^{(\ell)}\right)\sigma\left(z_{j;\alpha_{2}}^{(\ell)}\right)

Hence,

\Sigma_{\alpha\alpha}^{(\ell)}-\mathbb{E}\left[\Sigma_{\alpha\alpha}^{(\ell)}\right]=\frac{C_{W}}{n_{\ell}}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{\ell}}X_{j;\alpha},

where

X_{j;\alpha}:=\sigma\left(z_{j;\alpha}^{(\ell)}\right)^{2}-\mathbb{E}\left[\sigma\left(z_{j;\alpha}^{(\ell)}\right)^{2}\right].

Similarly

\partial_{x_{\alpha_{1}}}\Sigma_{\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}}^{(\ell)}\bigg|_{\alpha_{1}=\alpha_{2}=\alpha}-\mathbb{E}\left[\partial_{x_{\alpha_{1}}}\Sigma_{\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}}^{(\ell)}\right]\bigg|_{\alpha_{1}=\alpha_{2}=\alpha}=\frac{C_{W}}{n_{\ell}}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{\ell}}Y_{j;\alpha},

where

Y_{j;\alpha}=\sigma\left(z_{j;\alpha}^{(\ell)}\right)\sigma^{\prime}\left(z_{j;\alpha}^{(\ell)}\right)\partial_{x_{1;\alpha}}z_{j;\alpha}^{(\ell)}-\mathbb{E}\left[\sigma\left(z_{j;\alpha}^{(\ell)}\right)\sigma^{\prime}\left(z_{j;\alpha}^{(\ell)}\right)\partial_{x_{1;\alpha}}z_{j;\alpha}^{(\ell)}\right].

By symmetry, we obtain

\displaystyle\kappa_{(10)(00)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{n_{\ell}}\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1;\alpha}Y_{1;\alpha}\right]+C_{W}^{2}\left(1-\frac{1}{n_{\ell}}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1;\alpha}Y_{2;\alpha}\right].

We have by Theorem
3.2
that

\displaystyle\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{n_{\ell}}\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1;\alpha}Y_{1;\alpha}\right]=\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{n_{\ell}}\left[\left\langle\sigma^{3}\sigma^{\prime}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}-\left\langle\sigma^{2}\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}\left\langle\sigma\sigma^{\prime}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}\right]+O(n^{-2}),

giving the first term in (
10.11
). Further, note that
X_{1;\alpha}Y_{2;\alpha}
is a polynomial of total degree one in the derivative
d_{1}z_{i;\alpha}^{(\ell)}
. Hence, applying (
10.13
) and (
10.14
), we find that
C_{W}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1;\alpha}Y_{2;\alpha}\right]
equals

\displaystyle C_{W}^{2}\left\langle X_{1;\alpha}\right\rangle_{\kappa^{(\ell)}}\left\langle Y_{2;\alpha\alpha}\right\rangle_{\kappa^{(\ell)}}

plus

\displaystyle\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{4}\left[2\kappa_{(00)(00)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial z_{1}^{2}}X_{1;\alpha}\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial z_{2}^{2}}Y_{2;\alpha}\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}+2\kappa_{(10)(00)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial z_{1}^{2}}X_{1;\alpha}\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial z_{2}\partial d_{1}z_{2}}Y_{2;\alpha}\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}\right]

plus an error of size
O(n^{-2})
. Note that by Theorem
3.2
we have

\displaystyle\left\langle X_{1;\alpha}\right\rangle_{\kappa^{(\ell)}},\,\mathbb{E}\left[Y_{1;\alpha}\right]

\displaystyle=O(n^{-1}).

Hence,

\displaystyle C_{W}^{2}\left\langle X_{1;\alpha}\right\rangle_{\kappa^{(\ell)}}\left\langle Y_{2;\alpha}\right\rangle_{\kappa^{(\ell)}}

\displaystyle=O(n^{-2}).

Further,

\left\langle\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial z_{1}^{2}}X_{1;\alpha}\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial z_{2}^{2}}Y_{2;\alpha}\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}=\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{2})\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})d_{1}z\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}

and

\left\langle\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial z_{1}^{2}}X_{1;\alpha}\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial z_{2}\partial d_{1}z_{2}}Y_{2;\alpha}\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}=\left\langle\partial^{2}\sigma^{2}\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}\left\langle\partial(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}.

Thus, all together, we find

\displaystyle\kappa_{(10)(00)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{n_{\ell}}\left[\left\langle\sigma^{3}\sigma^{\prime}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}-\left\langle\sigma^{2}\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}\left\langle\sigma\sigma^{\prime}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{4}\kappa_{(00)(00)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{2})\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})d_{1}z\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{2}\kappa_{(10)(00)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{2})\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}\left\langle\partial(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}

\displaystyle+O(n^{-2}),

completing our derivation of (
10.11
).

∎

10.4
Recursions for Finite Width Corrections to 2nd Cumulant

We remind the reader of our standing notation. Namely, we fix a network input
x_{\alpha}\neq 0\in\mathbb{R}^{n_{0}}
and define

\kappa_{(ij)}^{(\ell)}:=\mathrm{Cov}\left(\partial_{x_{i;\alpha}}z_{i;\alpha}^{(\ell)},\,\partial_{x_{j;\alpha}}z_{i;\alpha}^{(\ell)}\right)=\partial_{x_{i;\alpha_{1}}}\partial_{x_{j;\alpha_{2}}}\kappa_{\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}}^{(\ell)}\bigg|_{x_{\alpha_{1}}=x_{\alpha_{2}}=x_{\alpha}},\qquad i,j=1,\ldots,n_{0}.

We also recall that

K_{(ij)}^{(\ell)}:=\lim_{n\rightarrow\infty}\kappa_{(ij)}^{(\ell)},\qquad S_{(ij)}^{(\ell)}:=\kappa_{(ij)}^{(\ell)}-K_{(ij)}^{(\ell)}.

The purpose of this section is to derive recursions for
S_{(ij)}^{(\ell)}
to leading order in
1/n
. We have

Proposition 10.3
.

We have

\displaystyle S_{(00)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{C_{W}}{8}\kappa_{4;\alpha}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{4}\sigma^{2}\right\rangle_{K_{(00)}^{(\ell)}}+\chi_{||;\alpha}^{(\ell)}S_{(00)}^{(\ell)}+O(n^{-2}).

(10.18)

Further,

\displaystyle S_{(10)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\chi_{||}^{(\ell)}S_{(10)}^{(\ell)}+K_{(10)}^{(\ell)}\frac{C_{W}}{2}S_{(00)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{3}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}

(10.19)

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{W}}{8}\left[\kappa_{(00)(00)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{4}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})d_{1}z\right\rangle_{\kappa^{(\ell)}}+4\kappa_{(10)(00)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{3}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})\right\rangle_{\kappa^{(\ell)}}\right]+O(n^{-2}).

Moreover,

\displaystyle S_{(11)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\chi_{\perp}^{(\ell)}S_{(11)}^{(\ell)}+\frac{C_{W}}{2}K_{(11)}^{(\ell)}S_{(00)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}+O(n^{-2})

(10.20)

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{W}}{2}\left(K_{(10)}^{(\ell)}\right)^{2}S_{(00)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{4}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}+2C_{W}K_{(10)}^{(\ell)}S_{(10)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{W}}{8}\bigg\{\kappa_{(00)(00)}^{(\ell)}\left(K_{(11)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{4}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}+\left(K_{(10)}^{(\ell)}\right)^{2}\left\langle\partial^{6}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+8\kappa_{(10)(00)}^{(\ell)}K_{(10)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{4}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}+4\left(2\kappa_{(10)(10)}^{(\ell)}+\kappa_{(11)(00)}^{(\ell)}\right)\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}\bigg\}.

Finally,

\displaystyle S_{(12)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\chi_{\perp}^{(\ell)}S_{(12)}^{(\ell)}+\frac{C_{W}}{2}S_{(00)}^{(\ell)}\left(K_{(12)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}+K_{(10)}^{(\ell)}K_{(20)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{4}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right)

(10.21)

\displaystyle+C_{W}\left(K_{(10)}^{(\ell)}S_{(20)}^{(\ell)}+K_{(20)}^{(\ell)}S_{(10)}^{(\ell)}\right)\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}+O(n^{-2})

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\bigg\{\kappa_{(00)(00)}^{(\ell)}\left(K_{(12)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{4}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}+K_{(10)}^{(\ell)}K_{(20)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{6}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+4\left(\kappa_{(10)(00)}^{(\ell)}K_{(20)}^{(\ell)}+\kappa_{(20)(00)}^{(\ell)}K_{(10)}^{(\ell)}\right)\left\langle\partial^{4}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}

\displaystyle\qquad+4\left(\kappa_{(12)(00)}^{(\ell)}+2\kappa_{(10)(20)}^{(\ell)}\right)\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}\bigg\}.

Proof.

The derivation of these recursion is very similar, so we provide the details for the most involved case of
S_{(12)}^{{}_{(\ell+1)}}.
By definition, we have

\displaystyle\kappa_{(12)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[d_{1}z_{i;\alpha}^{(\ell+1)}d_{2}z_{i;\alpha}^{(\ell+1)}\right]=C_{W}\mathbb{E}\left[\sigma^{\prime}(z_{i;\alpha}^{(\ell)})^{2}d_{1}z_{i;\alpha}^{(\ell)}d_{2}z_{i;\alpha}^{(\ell)}\right].

Applying Theorem
3.2
(or more precisely equations (
10.13
) -(
10.15
)) we therefore find

\displaystyle\kappa_{(12)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=C_{W}\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}zd_{2}z\right\rangle_{\kappa^{(\ell)}}+O(n^{-2})

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{W}}{8}\bigg\{\kappa_{(00)(00)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{4}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}zd_{2}z\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+4\kappa_{(10)(00)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{3}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{2}z\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}+4\kappa_{(20)(00)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{3}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+4\left(\kappa_{(12)(00)}^{(\ell)}+2\kappa_{(10)(20)}^{(\ell)}\right)\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}\bigg\}.

(10.22)

Next, integrating by parts yields

\displaystyle\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}zd_{2}z\right\rangle_{\kappa^{(\ell)}}

\displaystyle=\kappa_{(12)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{\kappa^{(\ell)}}+\kappa_{(10)}^{(\ell)}\kappa_{(20)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{\kappa^{(\ell)}}

\displaystyle=\kappa_{(12)}^{(\ell)}\left(\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}+\frac{1}{2}S_{(00)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}\right)

\displaystyle+\left(K_{(10)}^{(\ell)}+S_{(10)}^{(\ell)}\right)\left(K_{(20)}^{(\ell)}+S_{(20)}^{(\ell)}\right)\left(\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}+\frac{1}{2}S_{(00)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}\right).

Similarly, for
j=1,2
we find

\displaystyle\left\langle\partial^{4}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}zd_{2}z\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}

\displaystyle=K_{(12)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{4}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}+K_{(10)}^{(\ell)}K_{(20)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{6}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}+O(n^{-1})

\displaystyle\left\langle\partial^{3}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{j}z\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}

\displaystyle=K_{(j0)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{4}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}.

Substituting this into (
10.22
), comparing with the recursion (
10.8
), and recalling that
\kappa_{(j_{1}j_{2})(j_{1}^{\prime}j_{2}^{\prime})}^{{}_{(\ell)}}=O(n^{-1})
yields the recursion (
10.21
).
∎

11
Proof of Theorem
4.5

In this section we prove Theorem
4.5
. The key technical step will be to solve the recursions for all possible second and fourth cumulants of the value and partial derivatives of some component of the output of a random fully connected network with respect to two different components of the network input that were obtained in §
10
. Before diving into this part of the proof in §
11.2
, we give the proof of Theorem
4.5
modulo solving the recursions in §
10
.

11.1
Reduction of Theorem
4.5
to Cumulant Recursions from §
10

We first recall the setup of Theorem
4.5
. Namely, we fix a non-linearity
\sigma
satisfying (
2.1
) that belongs to the
K_{*}=0
universality class, as defined in §
4.2.2
, and consider a depth
L
random fully connected network with input dimension
n_{0}
, hidden layer widths
n_{1}=\cdots=n_{L}=n
, output dimension
n_{L+1}
and non-linearity
\sigma
. We assume this network is tuned to criticality (see §
4.1
). We then fix a network input
x_{\alpha}\neq 0
and seek to compute the averages

\mathbb{E}\left[\mathrm{Grad~Mean}^{(1)}\right]\qquad\text{and}\qquad\mathbb{E}\left[\mathrm{Grad~Var}^{(1)}\right]

of the empirical mean

\displaystyle\mathrm{Grad~Mean}^{(1)}=\frac{1}{n_{0}n_{1}}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{0}}\sum_{i=1}^{n_{1}}\left(\frac{\partial z_{q;\alpha}^{(L+1)}}{\partial W_{ij}^{(1)}}\right)^{2}

(11.1)

and the empirical variance

\displaystyle\mathrm{Grad~Var}^{(1)}=\frac{1}{n_{0}n_{1}}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{0}}\sum_{i=1}^{n_{1}}\left(\frac{\partial z_{q;\alpha}^{(L+1)}}{\partial W_{ij}^{(1)}}\right)^{4}-\left(\mathrm{Grad~Mean}^{(1)}\right)^{2}

(11.2)

of the squared gradients
(\partial z_{q;\alpha}^{{}_{(L+1)}}/\partial W_{ij}^{{}_{(1)}})^{2}
over all weights in layer
1
. To evaluate these quantities to leading order in
n,\ell
, note that by symmetry the mean of the expressions
\mathrm{Grad~Mean}^{(1)}
and
\mathrm{Grad~Var}^{(1)}
(
11.1
) and (
11.2
) are independent of the choice of
q
. Next, note that by the chain rule we have

\frac{\partial z_{q;\alpha}^{(L+1)}}{\partial W_{ij}^{(1)}}=x_{j;\alpha}\sigma^{\prime}(z_{i;\alpha}^{(1)})\frac{\partial z_{q;\alpha}^{(L+1)}}{\partial\sigma(z_{i;\alpha}^{(1)})}.

Hence, by symmetry,

\mathbb{E}\left[\mathrm{Grad~Mean}^{{(1)}}\right]=\frac{1}{n_{0}}\left|\left|x_{\alpha}\right|\right|^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sigma^{\prime}\left(z_{1;\alpha}^{(1)}\right)\frac{\partial z_{q;\alpha}^{(L+1)}}{\partial\sigma(z_{i;\alpha}^{(1)})}\right)^{2}\right].

Recalling that
\mathcal{F}^{(\ell)}
is the sigma algebra generated by all weights and biases in layers up to and including
\ell
, we may thus write

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathrm{Grad~Mean}^{{(1)}}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n_{0}}\left|\left|x_{\alpha}\right|\right|^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sigma^{\prime}\left(z_{1;\alpha}^{(1)}\right)\right)^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{\partial z_{q;\alpha}^{(L+1)}}{\partial\sigma(z_{i;\alpha}^{(1)})}\right)^{2}~\bigg|~\mathcal{F}^{(1)}\right]\right].

(11.3)

Observe that the conditional expectation in the previous line is precisely the square of the partial derivative of one component of the output in a random depth
L-1
network tuned to criticality with respect to one component of the input
\sigma_{\alpha}^{{}_{(\ell)}}
. In the notation (
10.2
) above, we therefore have

\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{\partial z_{q;\alpha}^{(L+1)}}{\partial\sigma(z_{1;\alpha}^{(1)})}\right)^{2}~\bigg|~\mathcal{F}^{(\ell)}\right]=\kappa_{(11)}^{(L-1)}.

We will see by combining Lemma
11.1
and Proposition
11.3
below that

\displaystyle\kappa_{(11)}^{(L-1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{C_{W}e^{-\gamma}}{n_{1}L}\left(1+O(L^{-1})\right)+O(n^{-2}),

where the implicit constant in
O(L^{-1})
is independent of
L,n
. Since at leading order in
n,L
this expression is a (non-random) constant, we write by a slight abuse of notation

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathrm{Grad~Mean}^{{(1)}}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n_{0}}\left|\left|x_{\alpha}\right|\right|^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sigma^{\prime}\left(z_{1;\alpha}^{(\ell)}\right)\right)^{2}\right]\kappa_{(11)}^{(L-1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n_{0}}\left|\left|x_{\alpha}\right|\right|^{2}\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{K^{(1)}}\kappa_{(11)}^{(L-1)}

(11.4)

where in the last step we use that pre-activations in layer
1
are iid Gaussian even at finite width. We now similarly simplify the mean of the empirical gradient variance. Namely, we first note that by symmetry we have the decomposition

\mathbb{E}\left[\mathrm{Grad~Var}^{(1)}\right]=I-II

where

\displaystyle I

\displaystyle:=\left(\frac{1}{n_{0}}\left|\left|x_{\alpha}\right|\right|_{4}^{4}-\frac{1}{n_{1}}\left(\frac{1}{n_{0}}\left|\left|x_{\alpha}\right|\right|^{2}\right)^{2}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sigma^{\prime}(z_{1;\alpha}^{(1)})\frac{\partial z_{q;\alpha}^{(L+1)}}{\partial\sigma(z_{1;\alpha}^{(1)})}\right)^{4}\right]

\displaystyle II

\displaystyle:=\left(\frac{1}{n_{0}}\left|\left|x_{\alpha}\right|\right|^{2}\right)^{2}\left(1-\frac{1}{n_{1}}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sigma^{\prime}(z_{1;\alpha}^{(1)})\sigma^{\prime}(z_{2;\alpha}^{(1)})\left(\frac{\partial z_{q;\alpha}^{(L+1)}}{\partial\sigma(z_{1}^{(1)})}\frac{\partial z_{q;\alpha}^{(L+1)}}{\partial\sigma(z_{2}^{(1)})}\right)^{2}\right)^{2}\right].

By conditioning on
\mathcal{F}^{(1)}
as before (and again using that at leading order in
n,L
the cumulants
\kappa_{(ij)(i^{\prime}j^{\prime})}^{(\ell)}
are independent of the initial condition at large
\ell
) we have

\displaystyle I

\displaystyle:=3\left(\kappa_{(11)(11)}^{(L-1)}+\left(\kappa_{(11)}^{(L-1)}\right)^{2}\right)\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{4}\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}\left(\frac{1}{n_{0}}\left|\left|x_{\alpha}\right|\right|_{4}^{4}-\frac{1}{n_{1}}\left(\frac{1}{n_{0}}\left|\left|x_{\alpha}\right|\right|^{2}\right)^{2}\right)

(11.5)

\displaystyle II

\displaystyle:=\left(2\kappa_{(12)(12)}^{(L-1)}+2\left(\kappa_{(12)}^{(L-1)}\right)^{2}+\kappa_{(11)(22)}^{(L-1)}+\kappa_{(11)}^{(L-1)}\kappa_{(22)}^{(L-1)}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\times\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{K^{(1)}}^{2}\left(\frac{1}{n_{0}}\left|\left|x_{\alpha}\right|\right|^{2}\right)^{2}\left(1-\frac{1}{n_{1}}\right).

(11.6)

Combining the expressions (
11.4
), (
11.5
), and (
11.6
), we find that

\displaystyle\frac{\mathbb{E}\left[\mathrm{Grad~Var}^{{(1)}}\right]}{\mathbb{E}\left[\mathrm{Grad~Mean}^{{(1)}}\right]^{2}}

\displaystyle=3\frac{\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{4}\right\rangle_{K^{(1)}}}{\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{K^{(1)}}^{2}}\left(\frac{n_{0}^{-1}\left|\left|x_{\alpha}\right|\right|_{4}^{4}}{(n_{0}^{-1}\left|\left|x_{\alpha}\right|\right|_{2}^{2})^{2}}-\frac{1}{n_{1}}\right)\left(\widehat{\kappa}_{(11)(11)}^{(L-1)}+1\right)

\displaystyle-\left(1-\frac{1}{n_{1}}\right)\left(2\widehat{\kappa}_{(12)(12)}^{(L-1)}+2\left(\widehat{\kappa}_{(12)}^{(L-1)}\right)^{2}+\widehat{\kappa}_{(11)(22)}^{(L-1)}+1\right),

plus errors of size
O(n^{-2})
, where we’ve abbreviated for
i,j\in\left\{1,2\right\}

\displaystyle\widehat{\kappa}_{(ii)(jj)}^{(\ell)}:=\frac{\kappa_{(ii)(jj)}^{(\ell)}}{\kappa_{(ii)}^{(\ell)}\kappa_{(jj)}^{(\ell)}},\qquad\widehat{\kappa}_{(12)(12)}^{(\ell)}:=\frac{\kappa_{(12)(12)}^{(\ell)}}{\kappa_{(11)}^{(\ell)}\kappa_{(22)}^{(\ell)}},\qquad\widehat{\kappa}_{(12)}^{(\ell)}:=\frac{\kappa_{(12)}^{(\ell)}}{\left(\kappa_{(11)}^{(\ell)}\kappa_{(22)}^{(\ell)}\right)^{1/2}}.

Appealing to Propositions
11.2
and
11.3
we conclude

\displaystyle\widehat{\kappa}_{(11)(11)}^{(L-1)}+1

\displaystyle=1+\frac{8L}{3n}+O(n^{-1})+O(n^{-2})

\displaystyle 2\widehat{\kappa}_{(12)(12)}^{(L-1)}+2\left(\widehat{\kappa}_{(12)}^{(L-1)}\right)^{2}+\widehat{\kappa}_{(11)(22)}^{(L-1)}+1

\displaystyle=1+\frac{8L}{3n}+O(n^{-1})+O(n^{-2}),

where the implicit constants in
O(n^{-1})
depend only on
\sigma
whereas the constant in
O(n^{-2})
depends on both
\sigma
and
L
. Combining these expressions with the following estimates completes the derivation of Theorem
4.5
, modulo solving the recursions for the
2nd
cumulants
\kappa_{(ij)}^{{}_{(\ell)}}
and the fourth cumulants
\kappa_{(i_{1}j_{1})(i_{2}j_{2})}^{{}_{(\ell)}}
, which we supply in the next section.
\square

11.2
Completion of Proof of Theorem
4.4
: 2nd, 4th Cumulants at Large Depth for the
K_{*}=0
Universality Class

In this section, we complete the proof of Theorem
4.5
by solving the 2nd and 4th cumulant recursion obtained in §
10
when
\sigma
belongs to the
K_{*}=0
universality class. We start in §
11.3
by solving the infinite width variance recursions for
K_{(ij)}^{{}_{(\ell)}}
. We then use this for solving to leading order in
1/n
the fourth cumulant recursions for
\kappa_{(ij)(i^{\prime}j^{\prime})}^{{}_{(\ell)}}
in §
11.4
. Finally, we combine in §
11.5
the results of the preceding two sections to solve the recursions for
S_{(ij)}^{{}_{(\ell)}}
again at leading order in
n,\ell
.

11.3
Two Point Recursions

We remind the reader of our standing notation. Namely, we let
\sigma
be an non-linearity from the
K_{*}=0
universality class (see §
4.2.2
). We also fix a network input
x_{\alpha}\neq 0\in\mathbb{R}^{n_{0}}
and define

K_{(ij)}^{(\ell)}:=\lim_{n\rightarrow\infty}\mathrm{Cov}\left(\partial_{x_{i;\alpha}}z_{1;\alpha}^{(\ell)},\,\partial_{x_{j;\alpha}}z_{1;\alpha}^{(\ell)}\right)=\partial_{x_{i;\alpha_{1}}}\partial_{x_{j;\alpha_{2}}}K_{\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}}^{(\ell)}\bigg|_{x_{\alpha_{1}}=x_{\alpha_{2}}=x_{\alpha}},\qquad i,j=1,\ldots,n_{0}.

The recursive description for
K_{(ij)}^{(\ell)}
was obtained in Lemma
10.1
. These recursions held for any non-linearity and any values of
C_{b},C_{W}
. The following Lemma records the large
\ell
behavior of
K_{(ij)}^{(\ell)}
when
\sigma
belongs to the
K_{*}=0
universality class and the network is tuned to criticality.

Lemma 11.1
.

We have

\displaystyle K_{(00)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{a\ell}+O_{\delta}(\ell^{-2+\delta})

(11.7)

\displaystyle K_{(10)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{C_{W}e^{-2\gamma}x_{1;\alpha}}{n_{0}\ell^{2}}\left(1+O(\ell^{-1})\right)

(11.8)

\displaystyle K_{(11)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{C_{W}e^{-\gamma}}{n_{0}\ell}\left(1+O(\ell^{-1})\right)+O\left(\frac{(\sigma_{1}^{(0)})^{2}}{n_{0}^{2}\ell^{3}}\right)

(11.9)

\displaystyle K_{(12)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{x_{1;\alpha}x_{2;\alpha}}{n_{0}^{2}\ell^{3}}\right)

(11.10)

plus errors of size
O(n^{-1})
.

Proof.

From Lemma
10.1
we have, up to errors of size
O(n^{-1})
, that

\displaystyle K_{(00)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=C_{W}\left\langle\sigma^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}

(11.11)

\displaystyle K_{(10)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\chi_{||}^{(\ell)}K_{(10)}^{(\ell)}

(11.12)

\displaystyle K_{(11)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=C_{W}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left(K_{(10)}^{(\ell)}\right)^{2}+\chi_{\perp}^{(\ell)}K_{(11)}^{(\ell)}

(11.13)

\displaystyle K_{(12)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=C_{W}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}K_{(10)}^{(\ell)}K_{(20)}^{(\ell)}+\chi_{\perp}^{(\ell)}K_{(12)}^{(\ell)}.

(11.14)

We already proved (
11.7
) in §
B
. Next, (
11.12
) yields

K_{(10)}^{(\ell+1)}=K_{(10)}^{(1)}\prod_{\ell^{\prime}=1}^{\ell}\chi_{||}^{(\ell^{\prime})}.

By direct computation we have

K_{(10)}^{(1)}=\lim_{n\rightarrow\infty}\mathbb{E}\left[z_{i;\alpha}^{(1)}d_{1}z_{i;\alpha}^{(1)}\right]=\frac{C_{W}}{n_{0}}x_{1;\alpha}.

Moreover,

\displaystyle\prod_{\ell^{\prime}=1}^{\ell}\chi_{||}^{(\ell^{\prime})}

\displaystyle=\prod_{\ell^{\prime}=1}^{\ell}\left(1-\frac{2}{\ell^{\prime}}+O((\ell^{\prime})^{-2})\right)

\displaystyle\exp\left[\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=1}^{\ell}\log\left(1-\frac{2}{\ell^{\prime}}+O((\ell^{\prime})^{-2})\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\exp\left[O(\ell^{-1})+-2\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=1}^{\ell}\frac{1}{\ell^{\prime}}\right]

\displaystyle=e^{-2\gamma}\ell^{-2}\left(1+O(\ell^{-1})\right),

where
\gamma
is the Euler-Mascheroni constant. This yields (
11.8
). Further,

C_{W}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left(K_{(10)}^{(\ell)}\right)^{2}=O\left(\frac{(\sigma_{1}^{(0)})^{2}}{n_{0}^{2}\ell^{4}}\right).

Hence, using Lemma
6.2
, we find to leading order in
\ell,n
that

K_{(11)}^{(\ell+1)}=K_{11}^{(1)}\prod_{\ell^{\prime}=1}^{\ell}\chi_{\perp}^{(\ell)}+O\left(\frac{(\sigma_{1}^{(0)})^{2}}{n_{0}^{2}\ell^{3}}\right)=K_{11}^{(1)}\prod_{\ell^{\prime}=1}^{\ell}\left(1-\ell^{-1}+O(\ell^{-2})\right)+O\left(\frac{(\sigma_{1}^{(0)})^{2}}{n_{0}^{2}\ell^{3}}\right)

Noting that

K_{11}^{(1)}=\frac{C_{W}}{n_{0}}

shows

K_{(11)}^{(\ell+1)}=K_{11}^{(1)}\prod_{\ell^{\prime}=1}^{\ell}\chi_{\perp}^{(\ell)}+O\left(\frac{(\sigma_{1}^{(0)})^{2}}{n_{0}^{2}\ell^{3}}\right)=\frac{C_{W}e^{-\gamma}}{n_{0}\ell}\left(1+O(\ell^{-1})\right)+O\left(\frac{(\sigma_{1}^{(0)})^{2}}{n_{0}^{2}\ell^{3}}\right),

completing the derivation of (
11.9
). The derivation of (
11.30
) is similar except that
K_{12}^{(1)}=0.

∎

11.4
Four Point Recursions

We remind the reader of our standing notation. Namely, we let
\sigma
be an non-linearity from the
K_{*}=0
universality class (see §
4.2.2
). We also fix a network input
x_{\alpha}\neq 0\in\mathbb{R}^{n_{0}}
and recall that

\displaystyle\kappa_{(ij)(i^{\prime}j^{\prime})}^{(\ell)}

\displaystyle:=\mathrm{Cov}\left(\partial_{x_{i;\alpha}}\partial_{x_{j;\alpha}}\Delta_{\alpha\alpha}^{(\ell)},\,\partial_{x_{i^{\prime};\alpha}}\partial_{x_{j^{\prime};\alpha}}\Delta_{\alpha\alpha}^{(\ell)}\right)

\displaystyle=\kappa\left(\partial_{x_{i;\alpha}}z_{1;\alpha}^{(\ell+1)},\,\partial_{x_{j;\alpha}}z_{1;\alpha}^{(\ell+1)},\,\partial_{x_{i^{\prime};\alpha}}z_{2;\alpha}^{(\ell+1)},\,\partial_{x_{j^{\prime};\alpha}}z_{2;\alpha}^{(\ell+1)}\right),

where
\Delta_{\alpha\alpha}^{(\ell)}
is defined in (
3.10
).

The recursive description for
\kappa_{(ij)(i^{\prime}j^{\prime})}^{(\ell)}
was obtained in Proposition
10.2
. These recursions held for any non-linearity and any values of
C_{b},C_{W}
. The following Lemma records the large
\ell
behavior of
\kappa_{(ij)(i^{\prime}j^{\prime})}^{(\ell)}
when
\sigma
belongs to the
K_{*}=0
universality class and the network is tuned to criticality.

Proposition 11.2
.

We have

\displaystyle\kappa_{(00)(00)}^{(\ell)}

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{3n\ell a^{2}}(1+O(\ell^{-1}))=\frac{2\ell}{3n}\left(K_{(00)}^{(\ell)}\right)^{2}(1+O(\ell^{-1}))

(11.15)

\displaystyle\kappa_{(10)(00)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{C_{W}e^{-2\gamma}x_{1;\alpha}}{3ann_{0}\ell^{2}}\left(1+O(\ell^{-1})\right)=\frac{\ell}{3n}K_{(10)}^{(\ell)}K_{(00)}^{(\ell)}\left(1+O(\ell^{-1})\right)

(11.16)

\displaystyle\kappa_{(10)(10)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{C_{W}e^{-\gamma}}{3ann_{0}\ell}(1+O(\ell^{-1}))=\frac{\ell}{3n}K_{(00)}^{(\ell)}K_{(11)}^{(\ell)}(1+O(\ell^{-1}))

(11.17)

\displaystyle\kappa_{(10)(20)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{x_{1;\alpha}x_{2;\alpha}}{nn_{0}^{2}\ell^{3}}\right)

(11.18)

\displaystyle\kappa_{(11)(00)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=-\frac{C_{W}e^{-\gamma}}{3ann_{0}\ell}(1+O(\ell^{-1}))=-\frac{\ell}{3n}K_{(11)}^{(\ell)}K_{(00)}^{(\ell)}(1+O(\ell^{-1}))

(11.19)

\displaystyle\kappa_{(12)(00)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{1}{nn_{0}^{2}\ell^{3}}\right)

(11.20)

\displaystyle\kappa_{(11)(10)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{1}{nn_{0}^{2}\ell^{2}}\right)

(11.21)

\displaystyle\kappa_{(12)(10)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{x_{2;\alpha}}{nn_{0}^{2}\ell}\right)

(11.22)

\displaystyle\kappa_{(11)(20)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{x_{2;\alpha}}{nn_{0}^{2}\ell}\right)

(11.23)

\displaystyle\kappa_{(11)(11)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{8C_{W}^{2}e^{-2\gamma}}{3nn_{0}^{2}\ell}(1+O(\ell^{-1}))=\frac{8\ell}{3n}\left(K_{(11)}^{(\ell)}\right)^{2}(1+O(\ell^{-1}))

(11.24)

\displaystyle\kappa_{(11)(22)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{2C_{W}^{2}e^{-2\gamma}}{3nn_{0}^{2}\ell}(1+O(\ell^{-1}))=\frac{2\ell}{3n}K_{(11)}^{(\ell)}K_{(22)}^{(\ell)}(1+O(\ell^{-1}))

(11.25)

\displaystyle\kappa_{(12)(12)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{C_{W}^{2}e^{-2\gamma}}{nn_{0}^{2}\ell}(1+O(\ell^{-1}))=\frac{\ell}{n}K_{(11)}^{(\ell)}K_{(22)}^{(\ell)}(1+O(\ell^{-1}))

(11.26)

plus errors of size
O(n^{-2})
. Here,
\gamma
denotes the Euler-Mascheroni constant.

Proof.

This proof is a long but straightforward calculation using Theorem
3.2
, Lemma
6.2
, and the estimates from Lemma
11.1
. We will spell this out for the first few recursions and then simply write the recursion and its solution. For example, Theorem
3.2
shows

\displaystyle\kappa_{(00)(00)}^{(\ell+1)}=\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{n_{\ell}}\left(\left\langle\sigma^{4}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}-\left\langle\sigma^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}^{2}\right)+\left(\chi_{||}^{(\ell)}\right)^{2}\kappa_{(00)(00)}^{(\ell)}

plus errors of size
O(n^{-2})
. A direction computation then yields

\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{n_{\ell}}\left(\left\langle\sigma^{4}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}-\left\langle\sigma^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}^{2}\right)=\frac{2}{n_{\ell}}\left(\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(00)}\right)^{2}\left(1+O(\ell^{-1})\right).

Hence, setting
n_{\ell}=n
we may apply Lemma
6.2
to obtain

\displaystyle\kappa_{(00)(00)}^{(\ell)}=\frac{2}{3n\ell a^{2}}(1+O(\ell^{-1})).

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\kappa_{(10)(00)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{n_{\ell}}\left(\left\langle\sigma^{3}\sigma^{\prime}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}-\left\langle\sigma^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\sigma\sigma^{\prime}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{8}\left[2\kappa_{(00)(00)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}\sigma^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}+4\kappa_{(10)(00)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}\sigma^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right]

plus errors of size
O(n^{-2})
. A direct computation shows that

\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{n_{\ell}}\left(\left\langle\sigma^{3}\sigma^{\prime}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}-\left\langle\sigma^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\sigma\sigma^{\prime}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right)=\frac{2\kappa^{(\ell)}_{00}\kappa^{(\ell)}_{10}}{n_{\ell}}\left(1+O(\ell^{-1})\right)

Hence, setting
n_{\ell}=n
and

\displaystyle\kappa_{(10)(00)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=-\frac{2C_{W}x_{1;\alpha}e^{-2\gamma}}{3ann_{0}\ell^{3}}\left(1+O(\ell^{-1})\right)+\left(1-\frac{4}{\ell}+O(\ell^{-2})\right)\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(10)(00)}.

Thus, Lemma
6.2
yields

\displaystyle\kappa_{(10)(00)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=-\frac{e^{-2\gamma}(x_{1;\alpha})^{2}}{3ann_{0}\ell^{2}}=O\left(\frac{x_{1;\alpha}}{nn_{0}\ell^{2}}\right).

Further, applying Theorem
3.2
gives

\displaystyle\kappa_{(10)(10)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{n_{\ell}}\left[\left\langle(\sigma\sigma^{\prime}d_{1}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}-\left\langle\sigma\sigma^{\prime}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{8}\left[2\kappa_{(00)(00)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})d_{1}z\right\rangle^{2}+\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(10)(00)}8\left\langle\partial(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\qquad\qquad+8\kappa_{(10)(10)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})\right\rangle_{(\ell)}^{2}\right].

A direct computation shows

\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{n_{\ell}}\left[\left\langle(\sigma\sigma^{\prime}d_{1}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}-\left\langle\sigma\sigma^{\prime}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}^{2}\right]=\frac{\kappa^{(\ell)}_{11}\kappa^{(\ell)}_{00}}{n}\left(1+O(\ell^{-1})\right).

Setting
n_{\ell}=n
and using the formulas above shows

\displaystyle\kappa_{(10)(10)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{\kappa^{(\ell)}_{11}\kappa^{(\ell)}_{00}}{n}\left(1+O(\ell^{-1})\right)+\left(1-\frac{4}{\ell}\right)\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(10)(10)}

and hence by Lemma
6.2
we see that

\displaystyle\kappa_{(10)(10)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{C_{W}e^{-\gamma}}{3nn_{0}a\ell}\left(1+O(\ell^{-1})\right).

Next, we obtain

\displaystyle\kappa_{(10)(20)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{n_{\ell}}\left[\left\langle(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}zd_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}-\left\langle\sigma\sigma^{\prime}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\sigma\sigma^{\prime}d_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{8}\left[\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(00)(00)}2\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})d_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(10)(00)}4\left\langle\partial(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})d_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}+\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(20)(00)}4\left\langle\partial(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\left.\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(10)(20)}8\left\langle\partial(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})\right\rangle_{(\ell)}^{2}\right].

Keeping the leading order in
\ell
we therefore have

\displaystyle\kappa_{(10)(20)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{x_{1;\alpha}x_{2;\alpha}}{nn_{0}^{2}\ell^{4}}\right)+\left(1-\frac{4}{\ell}+O(\ell^{-2})\right)\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(10)(20)}.

Thus,

\displaystyle\kappa_{(10)(20)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{x_{1;\alpha}x_{2;\alpha}}{nn_{0}^{2}\ell^{3}}\right)

Next,

\displaystyle\kappa_{(11)(00)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{n_{\ell}}\left[\left\langle(\sigma\sigma^{\prime}d_{1}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}-\left\langle\sigma^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime}d_{1}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{8}\left[2\kappa_{(00)(00)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}\sigma^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}(d_{1}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}+4\kappa_{(10)(00)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}\sigma^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\left.\kappa_{(11)(00)}^{(\ell)}4\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle\left\langle\partial^{2}\sigma^{2}\right\rangle\right].

We have

\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{n_{\ell}}\left[\left\langle(\sigma\sigma^{\prime}d_{1}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}-\left\langle\sigma^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime}d_{1}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right]=O\left(\frac{1}{nn_{0}\ell^{4}}\right).

Keeping the leading order in
\ell
gives

\displaystyle\kappa_{(11)(00)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\kappa^{(\ell)}_{11}\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(00)(00)}\frac{C_{W}}{2}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left(1+O(\ell^{-1})\right)+\chi_{\perp}^{(\ell)}\chi_{||}^{(\ell)}\kappa_{(11)(00)}^{(\ell)}

The solution is therefore

\displaystyle\kappa_{(11)(00)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=-\frac{2C_{W}e^{-\gamma}}{3ann_{0}\ell}\left(1+O(\ell^{-1})\right).

Next,

\displaystyle\kappa_{(12)(00)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{n_{\ell}}\left[\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\sigma^{2}d_{1}zd_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}-\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}zd_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\sigma^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{8}\left[\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(00)(00)}2\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}zd_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}\sigma^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(10)(00)}4\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}\sigma^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}+\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(20)(00)}4\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}\sigma^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\left.\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(12)(00)}4\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}\sigma^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right].

A direct computation shows that

\displaystyle\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{n_{\ell}}\left[\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\sigma^{2}d_{1}zd_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}-\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}zd_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\sigma^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right]=-a\kappa^{(\ell)}_{11}\left(\kappa^{(\ell)}_{00}\right)^{2}+O\left(\frac{1}{n_{0}^{2}n\ell^{4}}\right).

Thus, to leading order in
\ell
we have

\displaystyle\kappa_{(12)(00)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=-a\kappa^{(\ell)}_{11}\left(\kappa^{(\ell)}_{00}\right)^{2}\left(1+O(\ell^{-1})\right)+\chi_{||}^{(\ell)}\chi_{\perp}^{(\ell)}\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(12)(00)}

and we conclude that

\displaystyle\kappa_{(12)(00)}^{(\ell+1)}=O\left(\frac{1}{nn_{0}\ell^{2}}\right).

Next,

\displaystyle\kappa_{(11)(10)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{n_{\ell}}\left[\left\langle\sigma(\sigma^{\prime}d_{1}z)^{3}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}-\left\langle\sigma\sigma^{\prime}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime}d_{1}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{8}\left[\kappa_{(00)(00)}^{(\ell)}2\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}(d_{1}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\kappa_{(10)(00)}^{(\ell)}4\left(\left\langle\partial(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}(d_{1}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}+2\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\kappa_{(10)(10)}^{(\ell)}16\left\langle\partial(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}+\kappa_{(11)(00)}^{(\ell)}2\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\left.+\kappa_{(11)(10)}^{(\ell)}8\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right].

A direct computation shows that to leading order in
\ell

\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{n_{\ell}}\left[\left\langle\sigma(\sigma^{\prime}d_{1}z)^{3}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}-\left\langle\sigma\sigma^{\prime}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime}d_{1}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right]=\frac{\kappa^{(\ell)}_{10}\kappa^{(\ell)}_{11}}{n_{\ell}}=O\left(\frac{1}{n_{0}^{2}n\ell^{3}}\right).

We since all other terms are at this order in
\ell
or lower we obtain

\kappa_{(11)(10)}^{(\ell+1)}=O\left(\frac{1}{nn_{0}^{2}\ell^{2}}\right).

Next,

\displaystyle\kappa_{(12)(10)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{n_{\ell}}\left[\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{3}\sigma(d_{1}z)^{2}d_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}-\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}zd_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\sigma\sigma^{\prime}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{8}\left[\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(00)(00)}2\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}zd_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(10)(00)}4\left\{\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}+\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}zd_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(20)(00)}4\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}+\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(12)(00)}4\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(10)(10)}8\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})\right\rangle_{(\ell)}+\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(10)(20)}8\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})\right\rangle_{(\ell)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\left.+\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(12)(10)}8\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right].

A direct computation shows that to leading order in
\ell
we have

\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{n_{\ell}}\left[\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{3}\sigma(d_{1}z)^{2}d_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}-\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}zd_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\sigma\sigma^{\prime}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right]=O\left(\frac{x_{2;\alpha}}{nn_{0}\ell^{3}}\right)

and

\displaystyle\kappa_{(12)(10)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{x_{2;\alpha}}{nn_{0}^{2}\ell^{3}}\right)+\left(1-\frac{2}{\ell}+O(\ell^{-2})\right)\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(12)(10)}.

Hence,

\kappa_{(12)(10)}^{(\ell+1)}=O\left(\frac{x_{2;\alpha}}{nn_{0}^{2}\ell^{2}}\right).

Next,

\displaystyle\kappa_{(11)(20)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{n_{\ell}}\left[\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\sigma(d_{1}z)^{2}d_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}-\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime}d_{1}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\sigma\sigma^{\prime}d_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{8}\left[\kappa_{(00)(00)}^{(\ell)}2\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}(d_{1}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})d_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(10)(00)}8\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})d_{2}z\right\rangle+\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(20)(00)}4\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}(d_{1}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})\right\rangle_{(\ell)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(11)(00)}4\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})d_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}+\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(10)(20)}16\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})\right\rangle_{(\ell)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\left.\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(11)(20)}8\left\langle\partial(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right].

We therefore have

\displaystyle\kappa_{(11)(20)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{x_{1;\alpha}x_{2;\alpha}}{nn_{0}^{2}\ell^{3}}\right)+\left(1-\frac{4}{\ell}+O(\ell^{-2})\right)\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(11)(20)}.

Hence,

\displaystyle\kappa_{(11)(20)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{x_{1;\alpha}x_{2;\alpha}}{nn_{0}^{2}\ell^{2}}\right).

Next,

\displaystyle\kappa_{(11)(11)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{n_{\ell}}\left[\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime}dz)^{4}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}-\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime}dz)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{8}\left[\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(00)(00)}2\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}(d_{1}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}^{2}+\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(10)(00)}16\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}(d_{1}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad+\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(11)(00)}8\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}(d_{1}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}+\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(10)(10)}32\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\left.+\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(11)(10)}32\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}+\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(11)(11)}8\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{n_{\ell}}\left[\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime}dz)^{4}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}-\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime}dz)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+C_{W}^{2}\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(11)(00)}\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}(d_{1}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}+\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(11)(11)}\left(\chi_{\perp}^{(\ell)}\right)^{2}.

A direction computation shows that to leading order in
\ell

\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{n_{\ell}}\left[\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime}dz)^{4}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}-\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime}dz)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}^{2}\right]=\frac{2\left(\kappa^{(\ell)}_{11}\right)^{2}}{n_{\ell}}.

Hence, to leading order in
\ell
we simply have

\displaystyle\kappa_{(11)(11)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{2}{n_{\ell}}\left(\kappa_{11}^{(\ell)}\right)^{2}+(-2a)\kappa^{(\ell)}_{11}\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(11)(00)}\right)\left(1+O(\ell^{-1})\right)+\left(1-\frac{2}{\ell}\right)\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(11)(11)}.

We thus find

\displaystyle\kappa_{(11)(11)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{7C_{W}^{2}e^{-2\gamma}}{3nn_{0}^{2}\ell}\left(1+O(\ell^{-1})\right).

Next,

\displaystyle\kappa_{(11)(22)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{n_{\ell}}\left[\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{4}(d_{1}zd_{2}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}-\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime}d_{1}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime}d_{2}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{8}\left[\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(00)(00)}2\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}(d_{1}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}(d_{2}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\left(\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(10)(00)}+\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(20)(00)}\right)8\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(11)(00)}4\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}(d_{2}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}+\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(22)(00)}4\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})(d_{1}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(10)(20)}32\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(11)(20)}16\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}+\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(22)(10)}16\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\left.+\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(11)(22)}8\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}^{2}\right].

Substituting the solutions above we find

\displaystyle\kappa_{(11)(22)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{4C_{W}^{2}e^{-2\gamma}}{3nn_{0}^{2}\ell}\left(1+O(\ell^{-1})\right).

Finally,

\displaystyle\kappa_{(12)(12)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{n_{\ell}}\left[\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{4}(d_{1}zd_{2}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}-\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}zd_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{8}\left[\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(00)(00)}2\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}zd_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}^{2}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(10)(00)}8\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}zd_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}+\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(20)(00)}8\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}zd_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(12)(00)}8\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}zd_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}+\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(10)(20)}16\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(10)(10)}8\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}^{2}+\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(20)(20)}8\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(12)(10)}16\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}+\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(12)(20)}16\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\left.+\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(12)(12)}8\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}^{2}\right].

To leading order in
\ell
we have

\displaystyle\kappa_{(12)(12)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{n_{\ell}}\left[\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{4}(d_{1}zd_{2}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}-\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}zd_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}^{2}\right]\left(1+O(\ell^{-1})\right)+\left(\chi_{\perp}^{(\ell)}\right)^{2}\kappa^{(\ell)}_{(12)(12)}.

A direct computation shows

\frac{C_{W}^{2}}{n_{\ell}}\left[\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{4}(d_{1}zd_{2}z)^{2}\right\rangle_{(\ell)}-\left\langle(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}d_{1}zd_{2}z\right\rangle_{(\ell)}^{2}\right]=\frac{\kappa^{(\ell)}_{11}\kappa^{(\ell)}_{22}}{n}\left(1+O(\ell^{-1})\right).

Hence,

\displaystyle\kappa_{(12)(12)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{C_{W}^{2}e^{-2\gamma}}{nn_{0}^{2}\ell}\left(1+O(\ell^{-1})\right).

∎

11.5
Finite Width Corrections to 2nd Cumulant

We remind the reader of our standing notation. Namely, we let
\sigma
be an non-linearity from the
K_{*}=0
universality class (see §
4.2.2
). We also fix a network input
x_{\alpha}\neq 0\in\mathbb{R}^{n_{0}}
and recall that

\kappa_{(ij)}^{(\ell)}:=\mathrm{Cov}\left(\partial_{x_{i;\alpha}}z_{i;\alpha}^{(\ell)},\,\partial_{x_{j;\alpha}}z_{i;\alpha}^{(\ell)}\right)=\partial_{x_{i;\alpha_{1}}}\partial_{x_{j;\alpha_{2}}}\kappa_{\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}}^{(\ell)}\bigg|_{x_{\alpha_{1}}=x_{\alpha_{2}}=x_{\alpha}},\qquad i,j=1,\ldots,n_{0}.

The recursive description for
\kappa_{(ij)}^{(\ell)}
and its correction

S_{(ij)}^{(\ell)}:=\kappa_{(ij)}^{(\ell)}-K_{(ij)}^{(\ell)}

relative to the infinite width limit was obtained in Proposition
10.3
. These recursions held for any non-linearity and any values of
C_{b},C_{W}
. The following Lemma records the large
\ell
behavior of
\kappa_{(ij)}^{(\ell)}
when
\sigma
belongs to the
K_{*}=0
universality class and the network is tuned to criticality.

Proposition 11.3
.

Fix
\delta\in(0,1)
. We have

\displaystyle\kappa_{(00)}^{(\ell)}

\displaystyle=\left[1-\frac{\ell}{3n}+O(n^{-1})+O_{\delta}(\ell^{-1+\delta})+O(n^{-2})\right]K_{(00)}^{(\ell)}

(11.27)

\displaystyle\kappa_{(10)}^{(\ell)}

\displaystyle=\left[1+O\left(n^{-1}\right)+O(\ell^{-1})+O(n^{-2})\right]K_{(10)}^{(\ell)}

(11.28)

\displaystyle\kappa_{(11)}^{(\ell)}

\displaystyle=\left[1+\frac{\ell}{3n}+O(n^{-1})+O(\ell^{-1})+O\left(\frac{(\sigma_{1}^{(0)})^{2}}{n_{0}\ell^{2}}\right)+O(n^{-2})\right]K_{(11)}^{(\ell)}

(11.29)

\displaystyle\kappa_{(12)}^{(\ell)}

\displaystyle=O\left(\left[1+\frac{\ell}{n}+O(\ell^{-1})+O(n^{-2})\right]K_{(12)}^{(\ell)}\right),

(11.30)

where the implicit constants in the error terms
O(n^{-1}),O(\ell^{-1}),O((\sigma_{1}^{{}_{(0)}})^{2}/n_{0}\ell^{2}),O(\ell/n)
depend only on
\sigma
, the implicit constant in
O_{\delta}(\ell^{-1+\delta})
depends on
\delta
and
\sigma
, and the implicit constants in
O(n^{-2})
depend both on
\sigma
and on
\ell
.

Remark 11.4
.

The results of Proposition
11.3
make clear that the error terms
O(n^{-2})
must depend on
\ell
. For instance, note that
\kappa_{(00)}^{(\ell)}
must be non-negative and hence the
O(n^{-2})
term is negligible only if
n\gg\ell
.

Proof.

Recall that in Lemma
11.1
we already the large
\ell
behavior of

\displaystyle K_{(ij)}^{(\ell)}

\displaystyle:=\lim_{n\rightarrow\infty}\kappa_{(ij)}^{(\ell)},\quad i,j\in\left\{0,1,2\right\}.

Define

S_{(ij)}^{(\ell)}:=\kappa_{(ij)}^{(\ell)}-K_{(ij)}^{(\ell)},\qquad i,j\in\left\{0,1,2\right\}.

By direct computation and Theorem
3.2
we obtain

\displaystyle\kappa_{(00)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=C_{W}\mathbb{E}\left[\sigma(z_{i;\alpha}^{(\ell)})^{2}\right]=C_{W}\left\{\left\langle\sigma^{2}\right\rangle_{\kappa_{(00)}^{(\ell)}}+\frac{1}{8}\kappa_{4;\alpha}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{4}\sigma^{2}\right\rangle_{\kappa_{(00)}^{(\ell)}}+O(n^{-2})\right\}.

Comparing this with the recursion (
10.5
) and using that by Theorem
3.1
we know
\kappa_{4;\alpha}^{(\ell)}=O(n^{-1})
allows us to conclude

S_{(00)}^{(\ell)}=O(n^{-1}),

(11.31)

where at the moment the implicit constant in the error term depends in an unknown way on
\ell
. However, substituting this estimate back into the recursion for
\kappa_{(00)}^{{}_{(\ell+1)}}
and integrating by parts yields

\displaystyle S_{(00)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=C_{W}\left\{\left\langle\sigma^{2}\right\rangle_{\kappa_{(00)}^{(\ell)}}-\left\langle\sigma^{2}\right\rangle_{K_{(00)}^{(\ell)}}\right\}+\frac{C_{W}}{8}\kappa_{4;\alpha}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{4}\sigma^{2}\right\rangle_{K_{(00)}^{(\ell)}}+O(n^{-2})

\displaystyle=\frac{C_{W}}{8}\kappa_{4;\alpha}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{4}\sigma^{2}\right\rangle_{K_{(00)}^{(\ell)}}+\chi_{||;\alpha}^{(\ell)}S_{(00)}^{(\ell)}+O(n^{-2})

Using (
11.7
) we obtain

S_{(00)}^{(\ell+1)}=-\frac{2}{3na\ell}(1+O(\ell^{-1}))+\left(1-\frac{2}{\ell}\right)S_{(00)}^{(\ell)}+O(n^{-2}).

Noting that
S_{(00)}^{(1)}=0
and applying Lemma
6.2
, we conclude

S_{(00)}^{(\ell+1)}=-\frac{1}{3an}(1+O(\ell^{-1}))+O(n^{-2}),

where as usual the implicit constant in the
O(\ell^{-1})
error is independent of
n,\ell
but the constant in
O(n^{-2})
may depend on
\ell
. Hence, by (
11.7
) we find

S_{(00)}^{(\ell+1)}=\left(-\frac{\ell}{3n}(1+O(\ell^{-1}))+O(n^{-2})\right)K_{(00)}^{(\ell)}.

Combining this with the definition (
10.3
) of
S_{(00)}^{{}_{(\ell)}}
yields (
11.27
). To obtain (
11.28
), we proceed similarly. Namely, Theorem
3.2
, the estimate
S_{(00)}^{(\ell)}=O(n^{-1})
, and integration by parts yield

\displaystyle\kappa_{(10)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=C_{W}\mathbb{E}\left[\sigma(z_{i;\alpha}^{(\ell)})\sigma^{\prime}(z_{i;\alpha}^{(\ell)})d_{1}z_{i;\alpha}^{(\ell)}\right]

\displaystyle=C_{W}\left\{\left\langle\sigma\sigma^{\prime}d_{1}z\right\rangle_{\kappa^{(\ell)}}+\frac{1}{8}\left[\kappa_{(00)(00)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{4}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})d_{1}z\right\rangle_{\kappa^{(\ell)}}+2\kappa_{(10)(00)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{3}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})\right\rangle_{\kappa^{(\ell)}}\right]\right\}

\displaystyle=\kappa_{(10)}^{(\ell)}\left[\chi_{||}^{(\ell)}+\frac{C_{W}}{2}S_{(00)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{3}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}\right]+O(n^{-2})

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{W}}{8}\left[\kappa_{(00)(00)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{4}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})d_{1}z\right\rangle_{\kappa^{(\ell)}}+4\kappa_{(10)(00)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{3}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})\right\rangle_{\kappa^{(\ell)}}\right].

Hence,

\displaystyle S_{(10)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=S_{(10)}^{(\ell)}\left[\chi_{||}^{(\ell)}+\frac{C_{W}}{2}S_{(00)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{3}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}\right]+K_{(10)}^{(\ell)}\frac{C_{W}}{2}S_{(00)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{3}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{W}}{8}\left[\kappa_{(00)(00)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{4}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})d_{1}z\right\rangle_{\kappa^{(\ell)}}+4\kappa_{(10)(00)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{3}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})\right\rangle_{\kappa^{(\ell)}}\right]+O(n^{-2}).

This recursion shows that
S_{(10)}^{(\ell)}=O(n^{-1})
and hence we obtain

\displaystyle S_{(10)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\chi_{||}^{(\ell)}S_{(10)}^{(\ell)}+K_{(10)}^{(\ell)}\frac{C_{W}}{2}S_{(00)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{3}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{W}}{8}\left[\kappa_{(00)(00)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{4}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})d_{1}z\right\rangle_{\kappa^{(\ell)}}+4\kappa_{(10)(00)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{3}(\sigma\sigma^{\prime})\right\rangle_{\kappa^{(\ell)}}\right]+O(n^{-2}).

The asymptotics from the formula above for
S_{(00)}^{{}_{(\ell)}}
and Proposition
11.2
we see that

\kappa_{(10)(00)}^{(\ell)}=-K_{(10)}^{(\ell)}\frac{C_{W}}{2}S_{(00)}^{(\ell)}+O\left(\frac{1}{n\ell^{3}}\right).

Hence, we find all together that

\displaystyle S_{(10)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\left(1-\frac{2}{\ell}+O(\ell^{-2})\right)S_{(10)}^{(\ell)}+O\left(\frac{x_{1;\alpha}}{nn_{0}\ell^{3}}\right)+O(n^{-2})=O\left(\frac{x_{1;\alpha}}{nn_{0}\ell^{2}}\right)+O(n^{-2}).

Combining this with (
11.8
) confirms (
11.28
). We now proceed to deriving (
11.29
). By using Theorem
3.2
and integrating by parts

\displaystyle\kappa_{(11)}^{(\ell+1)}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(d_{1}z_{i;\alpha}^{(\ell+1)}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sigma^{\prime}(z_{i;\alpha}^{(\ell)})d_{1}z_{i;\alpha}^{(\ell)}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\kappa_{(11)}^{(\ell)}\left[\chi_{\perp}^{(\ell)}+\frac{C_{W}}{2}S_{(00)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}\right]+C_{W}\left(\kappa_{(10)}^{(\ell)}\right)^{2}\left[\left\langle\partial^{2}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}+\frac{1}{2}S_{(00)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{4}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{W}}{8}\left\{\kappa_{(00)(00)}^{(\ell)}\left[K_{(11)}^{(\ell)}\left\langle\partial^{4}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}+\left(K_{(10)}^{(\ell)}\right)^{2}\left\langle\partial^{6}(\sigma^{\prime})^{2}\right\rangle_{K^{(\ell)}}\right]\right.
